=== idx: 595 ===

LLM supporting:  the main regulatory molecule of serca2a function in the cardiac muscle is phospholamban ( pln ). pln directly interacts with serca2a and modulates its activity. under normal conditions, dephosphorylated pln inhibits serca2a, thereby reducing the uptake of calcium ions ( ca2 + ) into the sarcoplasmic reticulum ( sr ). this inhibition is crucial for maintaining the balance of intracellular ca2 + levels, which is essential for proper cardiac function. the phosphorylation of pln, particularly at the thr17 residue, by ca2 + - calmodulin - dependent protein kinase ( camkii ), or at ser16 by camp - dependent protein kinase, leads to the relief of this inhibition, thus increasing sr ca2 + uptake and enhancing the contractility of the heart. these regulatory mechanisms are particularly significant under various pathophysiological conditions, such as acidosis, ischemia / reperfusion injury, and heart failure, where the modulation of serca2a activity can have profound effects on cardiac function and recovery.
